## 2026-11-24 — FP-041 ACCEPTED: IBAN preflight old-auth save cannot dispatch in newer session

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 104/132 completed**, unchanged; accepted bounded I19.04/I22.02 owner-auth provenance correction, **not** completion of either original implementation. Draft [PR #654](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/pull/654) remains unmerged → `develop`.

**Confirmed race / fix:** `src/lib/accountMetadataClient.ts::saveAccountMetadata` started an IBAN update, then awaited a cold `refreshAccountMetadata(true)` before capturing the write's auth epoch. A previous-session delayed GET, retired by logout/MFA/new-session authentication, could later resolve with the fresh owner session's shared IBAN catalog. Without a provenance check, the old save continuation could send a `PUT /api/account-metadata` under the new session cookie and `If-Match` from the new owner's row. Server owner+AAL2, device session, CSRF, RLS and revision checks protected the **current** session but could not know that this browser action originated in a different authentication scope. [`52b5526`](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/commit/52b5526cba09a3c5d34996d2513a8a9778f0fbfa) captures the initiating auth epoch **before** shared save-progress notification or any asynchronous GET, rejects a retired action at the start and immediately after readback **before** reading a fresh revision or sending PUT. The old action fails definitively pre-dispatch without quarantining a non-existent write; only a new, explicitly initiated current-session save can PUT. The existing post-dispatch uncertain-write safeguards, valid receipt/revision confirmation, true 409 behavior, and server security remain unchanged. No UI markup/CSS, backend endpoint or SQL changes.

**Regression coverage:** New `tests/649-account-metadata-preflight-auth-scope.test.ts` adds **eight** direct client tests: four auth boundaries (`rheomiq:auth-expired`, `rheomiq:mfa-required`, `myfinhub:auth-completed`, `myfinhub:auth-scope-reset`) in the exact old GET/new GET interleaving, three old GET 401/503/transport-loss outcomes, and a synchronous auth reset from shared save-activity publish. Each requires **zero stale PUTs**, no accidental old 401 auth-expired side effect, preserved new owner catalog and red—not green—old write feedback; the explicit new owner save uses exactly `If-Match: 8`. Register prevention pattern **FP-041** in `quality/defect-patterns.json`; no token, IBAN financial data, credentials or customer secrets are committed (synthetic test data only).

**Exact source-head acceptance:** [CI #45298566145](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45298566145) **SUCCESS** on `52b5526cba09a3c5d34996d2513a8a9778f0fbfa`: `validate`, JavaScript/TypeScript CodeQL, Unified required gate all SUCCESS; **2,409/2,409 Vitest** in 343 files, local PostgreSQL PIN/RLS/recovery checks PASS, main JS **527.7 KiB raw / 160.0 KiB gzip** within the unchanged **528/165 KiB** budget. This is a client security/provenance guard, not a user-visible rendering change; the existing `/qa.html` synthetic metadata mode bypasses the real production PUT and cannot validate this race visually, so acceptance rests on the direct asynchronous client interleaving tests and exact source CI, **not** any unrun browser/hosted proof. Prior FP-040 user-visible desktop/mobile browser evidence is independently accepted at code SHA `954f42e`.

**Boundaries / next:** Original I19.04 cross-store uncertain-upload browser reload/inter-tab and Storage orphan recovery and I22.02 residual authenticated endpoint matrix remain open. No Android, hosted financial/Storage mutation, destructive migration, privileged auth/RLS change, release or merge. Proceed with the next evidenced unclosed P0 in a coherent Draft batch; do not rerun accepted Real Stack/browser suites without new risk evidence. This is a **documentation-only** checkpoint after the successful source CI; check new documentation-head CI separately before reporting its result.

## 2026-11-24 — FP-040 ACCEPTED: Card Vault previous-auth readback / mutation receipt isolation

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 104/132 completed**, unchanged. This is an **accepted bounded FP-040 prevention fix**, not completion of the original I22.02 or I19.04 checklists. Draft [PR #654](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/pull/654) targets `develop`; no merge, production release, hosted finance or Storage mutations, Android changes or destructive operation.

**Root cause / fix:** Prior asynchronous `POST /api/card-secrets` PAN/expiry/CVV reads, optional legacy IndexedDB CVV fallback, and `PUT/DELETE` encrypted vault write acknowledgments did not track browser auth-scope provenance. An old response could be accepted after a new owner/MFA/device auth scope; an old delayed HTTP 401 could incorrectly expire the new auth scope, and an old write receipt could falsely appear committed in the new session. The `src/lib/cardVaultClient.ts` change in [`4c5cef6`](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/commit/4c5cef6ba88c43503c37a972e3d1e9418af43fdc) retains the canonical `watchAuthScope` epoch, checks response/body after completion and **before** auth-expired side effects, rejects stale readback including late legacy local CVV, and converts old-scope save/delete receipts to `UnverifiedCardVaultWrite`, never automatic retry or success. Existing current-scope genuine HTTP 401 behavior, server owner+AAL2/device checks, RLS, finance revision, encryption and idempotence boundaries remain intact. Generic error copy does not disclose PAN/CVV. `tests/649-card-vault-auth-scope.test.ts` covers old/current reads and 401, deferred JSON, stale save/delete, late legacy CVV and safe messaging; FP-040 guard in `quality/defect-patterns.json`.

**Source and strict bundle acceptance:** The initial FP-040 source change exceeded the preexisting **528 KiB raw** eager main JS budget by a small margin despite passing tests. In [`6f72848`](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/commit/6f72848f7700bff39187ffa02bc758e95fb8701b), fourteen repeated named-export dynamic lazy page/feature wrappers were consolidated into a typed helper **without making routes eager or changing the budget**. Legacy syntax-specific source guards were updated in [`4449bc1`](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/commit/4449bc16e48a14c541c6d8088206e3eec4413b96) to prove both direct and typed `React.lazy` patterns retain literal dynamic imports and at least twelve route-lazy pages. Latest QA-only source head [`954f42e`](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/commit/954f42e4f541cf399475c98a64ed51b1f230774d) passes exact [CI #45288838192](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45288838192) **SUCCESS** (validate, CodeQL JS/TS, Unified). Test-only [`575a68b`](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/commit/575a68bce0b4f22162dd54c0055fdc766ff3ccce), [`57b305e`](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/commit/57b305ed10caf12639c149ceada56a6a44fce174), [`954f42e`](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/commit/954f42e4f541cf399475c98a64ed51b1f230774d) isolate a fresh synthetic Card Vault session and drain **all** pending old-owner reads only after dispatching `myfinhub:auth-scope-reset`; assertions require each old result discarded, empty PAN/CVV, generic accessible error and no extra POST from retry. Final one-shot [FB Focused Rendered QA #45291442218](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45291442218) **SUCCESS on exact code head `954f42e`**: `PASS: card-vault-runtime`, **Failed focused suites: 0**, no retry. **I22.02/I19.04 confirmed negative boundary:** `deleteRecurringServiceAsset` already requires a server-issued release path, acknowledged Storage DELETE, purge RPC and exact-key (including inactive) PostgreSQL GET. However, the post-purge owner+AAL2 predicate previously ran **only on ambiguous purge receipts and before readback**. Under a normal HTTP-200/void purge reply, RLS could hide an inactive retained registry row if owner/AAL2/device rights changed before GET. The server could wrongly treat `[]` as proof of purge and return `{ok:true}`. This candidate verifies current owner+AAL2 **after** exact-key readback on both acknowledged and ambiguous RPCs, fails closed on false/malformed/503 owner proof, preserves no blind second Storage DELETE, and leaves same-owner RLS, original SQL and finance revision unchanged. ## 2026-11-23 — I19.04 FP-032 generated provider image URL provenance (NEW CANDIDATE)

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 104/132 completed**, unchanged. Prior source+docs head [`71c3e69480a0ace34a9f3ca23855e50cc73fb49e`](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/commit/71c3e69480a0ace34a9f3ca23855e50cc73fb49e) [CI #45095881583](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45095881583) **SUCCESS** (`validate`, CodeQL and Unified). FP-031 recurring/provider same-turn duplicate upload guard was previously accepted with [real browser #45095500141](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45095500141). PR #654 remains Draft→develop, source dependency bot PRs #657/#658/#660 and production release Draft #645 open.

**New confirmed contract mismatch:** Server `server/accountMetadataStore.ts` generates keys `<provider>-asset-<12hex>`, stores new provider artwork at `financial-provider-assets/providers/<provider>/<assetKey>.<mime-extension>`, and returns the derived public `/storage/v1/object/public/financial-provider-assets/providers/...?...v` URL. `src/lib/financialProviderClient.ts` accepted otherwise well-formed 200 receipts with an entirely unrelated HTTPS URL and `providerCatalogMatchesWrite` accepted such a URL from its purportedly final full-catalog GET when role, MIME, size and key otherwise matched. A newly uploaded binary could thus falsely appear fully saved even when its browser image referenced another bucket, provider directory, key or MIME. This is *not* a license to reject or migrate curated legacy provider URLs, which are independent read-only catalog metadata.

**Bounded candidate:** Require the exact new-server-asset public Storage path, provider directory, key and MIME extension both at positive upload acknowledgement and at aggregate catalog readback **only for expected freshly uploaded keys**. Preserve existing allowed HTTPS/loopback hosts, legacy-curated catalog data, same-owner AAL2/RLS, app cache/unknown-write protection, multi-slot image reuse, idempotent assignment and the independent metadata POST acknowledgement contract. Add direct client negative cases for otherwise perfect 200 receipts pointing at wrong provider/key/bucket/MIME/extra nested path/unrelated legacy URL; add exact binary catalog URL negative cases while keeping existing source integrity assertions. Correct valid QA synthetic assets to follow actual server path and serve only those synthetic Storage paths via the provider Chromium runner's intercepted image requests. Transform the existing synthetic provider **HTTP-200 ambiguous binary** scenario to have valid key/role/bytes and a **foreign provider Storage URL**; the editor must retain its accessible warning, disabled repeat Save, zero binding writes and no false green, in desktop and mobile.

**Validation PENDING:** New exact-head `validate`, CodeQL, Unified, one targeted existing `<!-- run-649-provider-receipt-qa -->` provider branding/receipt Chromium suite only after source green, download and direct inspect evidence, then remove the marker and update branch checkpoint. No new SQL migration, hosted Storage/DB write/delete, Android, release or merge. Original I19.04 scope still incomplete; no counters advanced.

## 2026-11-23 — FP-031 I19.04 recurring/provider same-turn generated-asset upload race candidate

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 104/132 completed** (unchanged; broad I19.04/I22.02 not claimed complete). Previous exact docs head `3df51c834d8e74e0e44cf1fe274cd298e340afac` [CI #45093794579](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45093794579) **SUCCESS** (`validate`, CodeQL and Unified); earlier combined dependency boundary isolated [Real Stack #45092659037](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45092659037) and focused [shared controls #45093220360](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45093220360)/[desktop backup #45093220381](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45093220381) **SUCCESS** on accepted `0f1553d`. All PR-body QA markers are OFF. Do not rerun previously accepted expensive gates without a new boundary.

**Confirmed source-level P0 race:** `RecurringPage.save` depended on React `editBusy` state rather than a synchronous ref; two user Save clicks before repaint could both pass validation and launch separate `uploadRecurringServiceAsset` requests and finance saves. The *exported clients* `uploadRecurringServiceAsset` (random `service-asset-` key) and `uploadFinancialProviderAsset` (random `provider-asset-` key) likewise admitted simultaneous PUTs to the **same** recurring item or provider/role/variant slot, despite the provider page's own accepted ref latch. This could create duplicate registered binaries and orphan cleanup ambiguity even while the final finance revision has not acknowledged. An existing post-response uncertainty lock in the recurring client alone does **not** prevent two concurrent calls before the first response.

**Coherent source fix and regression proof staged (candidate, not yet CI-accepted):** Add synchronous per-recurring-item in-flight protection to recurring binary client and per-provider/role/variant protection to provider client. A second concurrent upload receives explicit in-progress error and **does not issue a second PUT**; genuinely different targets remain independent. Keep the lock through initial PUT **and catalog readback**, release on a settled same-scope result, and clear at auth-scope reset. An old-scope request's finally must not unlock the new auth scope. Preserve all previous 0/408/429/5xx uncertain receipt, safe 400 retry, direct owner/AAL2/RLS, catalog provenance, finance revision and explicit cleanup contracts. Add `RecurringPage` synchronous `editSaveInFlight` ref before the first async Storage or finance writer; release in its existing `finally`, preserving existing modal/busy/error copy. Extend **existing** provider/recurring Vitest suites with same-target no-second-PUT, distinct-target progress, definite-400 retry and retired-auth-scope-late-completion negatives. Extend existing finance asset source test with latch-order assertion. Add actual in-browser same-task **two-click recurring Save** to the existing recurring-service-brand Chromium suite, asserting the one-asset replacement remains **exactly one PUT** and preserves old asset cleanup/payment propagation. Register FP-031 prevention pattern; no new duplicate QA suite or parallel style owner.

**Validation:** New candidate requires exact-head normal `validate`, JS/TS CodeQL, Unified and **one** opt-in `<!-- run-649-recurring-asset-qa -->` focused Chromium once source green; inspect actual PNG/logs before accepting this user-visible race guard. Direct hosted/production PostgreSQL/Storage mutations, SQL migration, Android, release, main/develop merge, full Windows/WebKit/Lighthouse 216-image visual are **NOT** part of this candidate. Keep PR #654 Draft → develop. Counters unchanged. Next: inspect new CI, fix only evidenced failures in one coherent corrective batch, run one targeted rendered QA, remove the opt-in marker and reconcile acceptance; then proceed to remaining original #649 P0/P1.

## 2026-11-22 — #649 I19.04 provider archived-row lost-receipt false-green guard (new candidate)

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 101/132 completed** (unchanged; broader P0 I19.04/I22.02 and final review remain open). Previous exact [`d81073b`](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/commit/d81073b82eabc1872651d704eafc93e7c2796bf6) passed [CI #44995611080](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/44995611080) **SUCCESS** (validate/CodeQL/Unified) and one-shot isolated [Real Stack #44995649255](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/44995649255) **SUCCESS**, including genuine local GoTrue owner AAL1 and independently TOTP-verified nonowner AAL2 negative direct provider create/update RPCs, existing Storage/metadata/RLS checks, real Chromium and independent PostgreSQL+Storage restoration. The one-shot Real Stack PR marker was removed afterward. Full Ready Windows/WebKit/performance gates remain separate.

**New narrowly evidenced I19.04 risk:** After an ambiguous provider create/update RPC reply, `writeProviderRpc` verified owner+AAL2 and all six input fields but used an unfiltered provider-ID lookup. `rheomiq_update_financial_provider` explicitly refuses inactive rows, and `rheomiq_create_financial_provider` cannot reuse a colliding archived ID. The new provider asset CHECK originally used `storage_bucket = 'financial-provider-assets'`; a direct owner+AAL2 update could mark a generated asset inactive and set its bucket NULL, producing SQL UNKNOWN and bypassing expected immutable provenance checks. Use `coalesce(storage_bucket,'') = 'financial-provider-assets'` to fail closed for generated keys even if inactive. Add an actual direct PostgREST negative `PATCH {active:false,storage_bucket:null}` expecting 23514, and source guard for the predicate. Existing curated/non-generated keys remain exempt; no destructive migration, grant/RLS/role change, finance history change or hosted operation. All added checks remain **pending exact new-head source+CodeQL and isolated PostgreSQL/Storage Real Stack acceptance**; prior run #44647883848 failed in unrelated vault backup substring proof before reaching provider checks. **Confirmed cross-session race:** The three independent long-lived browser modules (`accountMetadataClient`, `financialProviderClient`, `recurringServiceAssetClient`) cached owner-scoped metadata after logout, MFA downgrade or subsequent successful sign-in. More seriously, an older pending owner-AAL2 GET could fulfill *after* a scope boundary and repopulate IBANs/custom provider names/image entries into a new anonymous/MFA context. Pending old GET responses cannot overwrite newer records. IBAN and recurring asset PUT/DELETE also refuse to publish success from a pre-boundary operation; no duplicate uploads, destructive deletes, direct production Auth mutation or token disclosure. `useSession` dispatches a generic nonsecret reset on genuine unauthenticated session/401/successful logout; prior auth-expired/MFA-required/auth-completed signaling and MFA/AAL2 guards retained.

**Focused verification:** New `tests/649-auth-scoped-metadata-caches.test.ts` exercises four auth boundaries × three independent stale-inflight GET stores, including reset before an old response resolves and acceptance of a later current GET, plus source guard on logout and QA trigger. ## 2026-11-19 — #649 I19.04 batch: provider RPC exact receipts, binding GET verification, IBAN cold/stale readback preflight

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 101/132 completed** (unchanged: I19.04 remains PARTIAL; no double count). Preceding exact [CI #44586310982](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/44586310982) at `2a1902e` **SUCCESS**: 327 Vitest suites/2052 tests, TypeScript/build/privacy/bundle, disposable PostgreSQL, CodeQL and Unified. Single implementation PR #654 Draft → develop; archived #650/#651/#653 and separate issues #646/#648/#652 already closed by owner-requested consolidation.

**Confirmed related false-green boundaries:**
1. `writeProviderRpc` previously returned the first row from any nonempty PostgREST RPC reply without comparing the actual ID/display/shortName/type/country/order against the submitted create/update. HTTP 200 with the wrong or duplicated row could be treated as a confirmed persistence receipt. It now requires exactly one row and exact submitted fields; otherwise **503 FINANCIAL_PROVIDER_WRITE_UNVERIFIED** (and malformed records retain strict schema failure). There is no automatic replay of a non-idempotent create.
2. The `rheomiq_set_financial_provider_asset_binding` RPC **returns void** and a plain HTTP 200/null is insufficient confirmation of a specific slot. Server now reads exactly that provider/role/variant via authenticated RLS after the RPC and requires the matching key (or no row for an explicit clear) before reporting `ok:true` to clients. Malformed/duplicate/unavailable readback becomes an uncertain failed receipt, not a green save.
3. Browser IBAN `saveAccountMetadata` previously used revision zero on a cold/failed account catalog, potentially causing a partial finance/metadata write; it now performs one bounded, read-only GET preflight when snapshot is cold/loading/error. If readback remains unverified, no PUT is issued. Verified existing revision is used in If-Match, and the prior exact server/client write receipt guard remains active.

**Regression:** New real mocked provider-RPC and binding-RPC+GET suite: eleven provider create/update/malformed/duplicate fixtures and eight binding-assignment/clear/ambiguity/unavailable fixtures. Expanded IBAN browser client scenarios: cold GET, stale/failed GET never POSTs and current revision 4 produces If-Match 4/next revision 5. These are focused I19.04 server/client finance-adjacent changes, not UI redesign, migrations or a new SQL function. No live hosted Supabase or Storage mutation, local/private finance data, main, release, Android or develop merge.

**Validation:** New candidate needs its own exact-head source `validate`, CodeQL and Unified result; previously successful head cannot be used to claim acceptance. Draft targeted suites/ordinary CI are sufficient for this source-only batch; defer Windows/WebKit/Lighthouse/full rendered/real stack to final coherent Ready head and verify relevant failure/recovery states there. ## 2026-11-19 — I18.05 connected device same-turn mutation latch and stale read suppression (source/browser candidate)

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 99/132 completed (unchanged, I18.05 not yet accepted).** Exact previous [CI #44545256452](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/44545256452) at `ef1e19f` **SUCCESS** (validate, CodeQL, Unified required gate, strict budget, local PostgreSQL). No other branch owners were changed.

**Confirmed interaction race:** `DeviceAccessSettings` relied exclusively on asynchronous `setBusy(true)` to disable follow-up confirmations. A second confirm event in the same synchronous turn could trigger an independent duplicate revoke/rename call: the later 404 or failed response could overwrite the genuine outcome. The owner-controlled remote session may then look unrevoked even after a successful server change. Introduce a synchronous `useRef` mutation latch around revoke-one, revoke-others and nickname saves; keep all existing owner/AAL2/Origin and actual server authorization unchanged. Use a request generation counter so a GET read started before mutation cannot overwrite its post-mutation acknowledged device list or display old read errors. Invalidate any outstanding GET and clear its loading spinner when starting mutation; preserve accurate red errors and only show green after verified mutation + resulting list.

**Targeted regression:** existing `tests/device-access.test.ts` protects latch, read-order and receipt rules. Existing `scripts/settings-tabs-qa.mjs` desktop synthetic device-revoke error journey now double-clicks the confirmation in the same JS task, and asserts exactly **one** POST through QA-only `qa.html` counter, while retaining original busy/error/visible screenshot evidence; no real device revoked or private user metadata. Run this one focused Settings browser suite and inspect screenshots only after source CI passes. No full release/Windows/WebKit/Android/production QA at Draft. New source head unvalidated; don't count I18.05 or claim the browser proof yet.

## 2026-11-19 — I19.03 bound receipt image decode geometry before local canvas work (candidate)

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 99/132 completed (unchanged; accepted I19.03 boundary is being extended, not recounted).** Exact prior head `440b708` [CI #44537663235](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/44537663235) validate/CodeQL/Unified SUCCESS and strict 528.0 KiB main-JS limit PASS. The existing 12 MiB image-file cap and magic-byte checks do not bound a highly compressed picture's decoded pixel area. Added 64 Mi-pixel/16,384-side ceiling before decoding declared PNG IHDR and after bitmap decoding for JPEG/OCR; realistic 48-MP phone images still normalize to the established local 2,400-pixel output, no server upload or stored schema change. The early PNG check requires the standard 13-byte first IHDR header, rejects zero/oversized dimensions before expensive decoder allocation; both normalized-image and OCR bitmap paths always close rejected bitmaps and do not allocate canvases. ## 2026-11-19 — I19.03 same-transaction local receipt quota and stale-update race

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 99/132 completed (unchanged; I19.03 previously accepted, this is a source-supported adjacent defect class).** Earlier exact `22e9b87` CI #44530723102 **validate SUCCESS**, including actual disposable PostgreSQL changed-event+configuration-cent matrix, root/API npm audit, tests, TypeScript/build/strict privacy/bundle; CodeQL/Unified were still in progress at that inspection and must not be claimed accepted without result.

**Confirmed local-only IndexedDB race:** `createReceiptDraft` read 30-draft/60-MiB quota in a readonly transaction and inserted later in a separate readwrite transaction; two concurrent captures (including different tabs) could each pass stale quota. `saveReceiptProposal` and `markReceiptDraftError` likewise read before a separate write and could recreate a receipt deleted between operations. Move each read/check/write into its **one serialized IndexedDB readwrite transaction** with unchanged count/byte caps, error copy, OCR privacy/no-upload semantics and finance-then-local-cleanup contract. Remove the now-unused independent read helper. Narrow regression source locks create quota and both update/delete race boundaries. No new capture UX, no synthetic finance writes or external data. **New source candidate not validated on head**; exact source/CodeQL/Unified needed, bounded receipt runtime/browser verification when final coherent QA is run. PR #654 remains Draft, no main, hosted data, Android or release.

## 2026-11-18 — I19.01 rapid row-identity correction for multi-source purchases and split expenses (source/browser candidate)

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 99/132 completed (unchanged: I19.01 remains PARTIAL; I19.02 and I19.07 already accepted).** Previous exact commit `7be4f737` [CI #44372133966](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/44372133966) **SUCCESS**: 1,950/1,950 Vitest, TS/build/privacy/bundle/local PostgreSQL, CodeQL/Unified and both root/API mandatory npm audits zero vulnerabilities on that head. [Focused Chromium #44372360161](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/44372360161) accepted one synthetic deferred-credit-vault reveal across six card rotations with a masked screenshot, and earlier debit Card Vault unmount/copy browser proof remains accepted. Draft opt-in off, no repeated card validation.

**Confirmed same-owner defect:** Quick Entry assigned newly added multi-funding rows `funding-${Date.now()}` and new split subparts `p${Date.now()}`. Rapid Add actions in the same millisecond could create duplicate React keys and, critically, a shared `row.id` in the funding update map and split raw-amount text map. Editing one funding row could change both; a split row's raw amount could appear in another row. That can misrepresent an owner-entered purchase draft before validation even though the canonical multi-funding engine rejects duplicate source IDs.

**Bounded correction:** One instance-local monotonic draft row allocator, reused by both editor sections, allocates outside React functional state updaters and skips IDs already present (including imported split part IDs). No finance event IDs, canonical ledger legs, economic-event counts, backend/schema/auth/Storage or edit-provenance contracts are changed. Dedicated source regression locks both consumers and the elimination of Date.now-derived row IDs. The existing `action-center-context` Chromium suite simulates two rapid same-timestamp Add clicks in each editor, edits the third of four rows, requires the fourth remain independent, confirms zero posted finance events, and captures 375px mobile screenshots with no document overflow. Existing invalid-cent and focus/error QA remains intact.

**Validation boundary:** new source, QA and tests NOT YET PASSED on this candidate; one targeted existing focused browser QA (prefer `action-center-context` alone) and direct inspection of the two mobile images/logs required before acceptance. No increment until entire I19.01 scope accepted; final Ready WebKit/Windows/Lighthouse/recovery and standalone dependency PR #660 reconciliation pending.

**Integration/release:** Draft PR #654 `fix/649-bulk-remediation` → `develop`; no merge/main/production, hosted DB/Storage, migration or Android changes. **Next:** inspect automatic exact-head source CI once, enable one scoped action-center-context QA using existing Draft opt-in or narrow selector, accept only after logs/screenshots; keep opt-in OFF again and proceed to next unblocked #649 source work in a coherent batch.
